**Found by:** verification ([TC-FILE-01](#tc-file-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** Anyone can request `/encryptionkeys/``<name>`, `/support/logs/``&lt;name&gt;` or `/ftp/quarantine/``&lt;name&gt;`; each handler only rejects slashes and sends the file, exposing signing keys, access logs and quarantined files.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  12 │
  13 │     if (!file.includes('/')) {
→ 14 │       res.sendFile(path.resolve('encryptionkeys/', file))
  15 │     } else {
  16 │       res.status(403)
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-057](#m-057) — Remove public serving of key, log and quarantine directories

**Classification:** Unauthenticated Management Plane · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-552](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/552.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-FILE-01](#tc-file-01)

<a id="t-045"></a><a id="f-045"></a>
#### F-045 · XML external entity expansion in uploaded XML parsing

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `lib/xml.ts:38`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-INPUT-06](#tc-input-06))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** A caller uploads an XML file to `/file-upload` with a DTD declaring an external entity such as `file:///etc/passwd`. The parser substitutes entities and loads the DTD, and the result is echoed in the error message.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  36 │   const sandbox = { libxml2, data, option }
  37 │   vm.createContext(sandbox)
→ 38 │   const xmlDoc = vm.runInContext('libxml2.XmlDocument.fromString(data, { option })', sandbox, { timeout: timeoutMs })
  39 │   const xmlString = xmlDoc.toString()
  40 │   xmlDoc.dispose()
```

**Fix:** Disable external entity resolution on every XML parser and reject DOCTYPE declarations → ◕ [M-058](#m-058) — Disable entity substitution and DTD loading

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-06](#tc-input-06)

<a id="t-046"></a><a id="f-046"></a>
#### F-046 · Any authenticated user can read any user record by id

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:365`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-1](#tb-1) 🌐 External - external → Juice Shop Express API · Authorization: The finding's evidence line is the interface this boundary cites; CWE-639 breaks its authorization condition.

**Issue:** GET `/api/Users/:id` (`server.ts:365`) is guarded only by `isAuthorized`, which verifies a valid JWT and does not compare :id with the token's user. Any logged-in user, such as a customer, can enumerate ids and read other accounts' records through the generated finale REST handler, including email, role, `deluxeToken` and last login IP.

One part notes the resource excludes password and `totpSecret` but nothing else; another did not read which fields the handler returns.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  363 │   app.get('/api/Users', security.isAuthorized())
  364 │   app.route('/api/Users/:id')
→ 365 │     .get(security.isAuthorized())
  366 │     .put(security.denyAll())
  367 │     .delete(security.denyAll())
```

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◕ [M-059](#m-059) — Enforce ownership on user lookup by id

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-047"></a><a id="f-047"></a>
#### F-047 · Server-side request forgery in profile

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/profileImageUrlUpload.ts:24`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-05](#tc-input-05))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-6](#tb-6) - Juice Shop Express API → external · Egress destination: The `imageUrl` destination is fetched with no public-address restriction at the egress crossing.

**Issue:** An authenticated user posts an `imageUrl`, such as an internal or cloud metadata address, to `/profile/image/url` (`routes/profileImageUrlUpload.ts:24`). The server fetches it with no scheme, host or private-address restriction and writes the response body into the public uploads directory, where the attacker downloads it.

Only a cookie login check applies.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  22 │       if (loggedInUser) {
  23 │         try {
→ 24 │           const response = await fetch(url)
  25 │           if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
  26 │             throw new Error('url returned a non-OK status code or an empty body')
```

**Fix:** Validate the URL scheme + host against an explicit allow-list before issuing outbound requests → ◕ [M-060](#m-060) — Validate and allowlist outbound request targets

**Classification:** Server-Side Request Forgery · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-918](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/918.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-05](#tc-input-05)

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:203`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-014](#w-014) - Unauthenticated chat endpoint lacks consumption limits (`chat.ts:203`)

**Issue:** POST `/rest/chat` has no authentication, no rate limit and no bound on message count or size; each request can trigger up to 10 model steps and tool queries. An attacker floods the endpoint to exhaust provider quota and server resources (LLM10, ASI08).

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  201 │
  202 │     try {
→ 203 │       const result = streamText({
  204 │         model: provider(model),
  205 │         system: systemPrompt,
```

**Fix:** Bound the request rate and the per-request resource budget on this endpoint → ◕ [M-061](#m-061) — Add rate limiting and token budgets to chat

**Classification:** Denial of Service · STRIDE: Denial of Service · [CWE-770](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/770.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/)

**Issue:** A logged-in user calls POST `/rest/basket/`&lt;other id&gt;`/checkout`. The basket is found by id with no owner comparison. The wallet debit and bonus credit use the client-supplied `req.body.UserId`, so the caller can charge another user's wallet.


**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

#### F-050 · Any user can edit other users' product

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/updateProductReviews.ts:18`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An authenticated user PATCHes `/rest/products/reviews` with another review's id (or an operator object) and a new message. The handler updates by body id without comparing the author, and `multi:true` lets one request rewrite many reviews.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  16 │     const user = security.authenticatedUsers.from(req) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line forgedReviewChallenge
  17 │     db.reviewsCollection.update( // vuln-code-snippet neutral-line forgedReviewChallenge
→ 18 │       { _id: req.body.id }, // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line noSqlReviewsChallenge forgedReviewChallenge
  19 │       { $set: { message: req.body.message } },
  20 │       { multi: true } // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line noSqlReviewsChallenge
```

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◕ [M-063](#m-063) — Bind review updates to the authenticated author

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** Any anonymous client requests `/encryptionkeys/:file`, `/support/logs/:file` or `/ftp/quarantine/:file`. The handlers send the file with no authentication or role check, disclosing key material, access logs and quarantined files.

<a id="t-062"></a><a id="f-062"></a>
#### F-062 · Coupon tool discount policy enforced only in prompt

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:184`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** The 10% cap and eligibility rules exist only in the system prompt and a schema description. The zod schema has no max and `execute()` calls `security.generateCoupon(discount)` with any model-supplied number.

An attacker prompt-injects the chatbot into calling `generateCoupon` with discount 90 and receives a valid coupon (LLM06, ASI02).

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  182 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 10) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
  183 │           challengeUtils.solveIf(challenges.chatbotGreedyInjectionChallenge, () => discount >= 50) // vuln-code-snippet hide-line
→ 184 │           const couponCode = security.generateCoupon(discount) // vuln-code-snippet vuln-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  185 │           return { couponCode, discount } // vuln-code-snippet neutral-line chatbotPromptInjectionChallenge
  186 │         }
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-071](#m-071) — Enforce coupon limits in server code

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-863](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/863.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-063"></a><a id="f-063"></a>
#### F-063 · Registration accepts client-supplied role

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:420`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Middleware does not strip `body.role`, but whether the User model or finale create accepts role is not shown in the supplied source.

**Weakness:** [W-003](#w-003) - Privileged attributes lack an enforced write allowlist

**Issue:** At `server.ts:420`, the POST `/api/Users` middleware trims fields but does not remove or reject `body.role`. `registerAdminChallenge` only records the attempt when role is admin and then calls next, and the generated User resource creates the account.

The user model's role handling was not served, so this is not fully confirmed.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
  418 │     next()
  419 │   })
→ 420 │   app.post('/api/Users', verify.registerAdminChallenge())
  421 │   app.post('/api/Users', verify.passwordRepeatChallenge()) // vuln-code-snippet hide-end
  422 │   app.post('/api/Users', verify.emptyUserRegistration())
```

**Fix:** ◑ [M-009](#m-009) — Manual review: verify Registration accepts client-supplied role · ◕ [M-072](#m-072) — Strip role from registration input

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03)

<a id="t-017"></a><a id="f-017"></a>
#### F-017 · Non-cryptographic RNG for a secret/token

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `lib/insecurity.ts:53`

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: `Math.random` is a non-cryptographic secret in `denyAll`, but its purpose is to reject all tokens. Predictability enabling a forged token is not shown.

**Weakness:** [W-036](#w-036) - Security-sensitive data uses weak cryptographic primitives

**Issue:** A predictable token/secret lets an attacker guess or brute-force session identifiers, reset links, or OTPs.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
  51 │
  52 │ export const isAuthorized = () => expressJwt(({ secret: publicKey }) as any)
→ 53 │ export const denyAll = () => expressJwt({ secret: '' + Math.random() } as any)
  54 │ export const authorize = (user = {}) => jwt.sign(user, privateKey, { expiresIn: '6h', algorithm: 'RS256' })
  55 │ export const verify = (token: string) => token ? (jws.verify as ((token: string, secret: string) => boolean))(token, publicKey) : false
```

**Fix:** Switch to a cryptographically secure RNG (`crypto.randomBytes` / OS `/dev/urandom`) → ◑ [M-003](#m-003) — Manual review: verify Non-cryptographic RNG for a secret/token · ◕ [M-034](#m-034) — Use cryptographically secure random values

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-CRYPTO-02](#tc-crypto-02) · Rules: CRYPTO-002

<a id="t-064"></a><a id="f-064"></a>
#### F-064 · Missing role check on user list endpoint

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `routes/authenticatedUsers.ts:12`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** Any authenticated low-privileged user can call GET `/rest/user/authentication-details` (`routes/authenticatedUsers.ts:12`) and receive every user's record, including email, role, deluxe token, last login IP and login times, because only `isAuthorized` (a valid JWT) guards the route and the handler uses `UserModel.findAll()`. Password and `totpSecret` are masked, other fields are not.

This enables targeting of privileged accounts.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  10 │ async function retrieveUserList (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  11 │   try {
→ 12 │     const users = await UserModel.findAll()
  13 │
  14 │     res.json({
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-073](#m-073) — Restrict user listing to admin role

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-037"></a><a id="f-037"></a>
#### F-037 · Stored XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/about/about.component.ts:119`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker submits feedback whose comment contains script-bearing HTML through the unauthenticated feedback endpoint. The About page (`frontend/src/app/about/about.component.ts:119`) concatenates the comment into markup, marks it trusted with `bypassSecurityTrustHtml`, and binds it with `innerHTML`, so the markup executes in every visitor's browser and can read the JWT in `localStorage`.

No client-side control exists; server-side feedback sanitization was not inspected.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```typescript
  117 │             feedbacks[i].comment
  118 │           }</p><div class="feedback-stars">(${this.stars[feedbacks[i].rating]})</div></figcaption>`
→ 119 │           feedbacks[i].comment = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(
  120 │             feedbacks[i].comment
  121 │           )
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◕ [M-050](#m-050) — Remove sanitizer bypass and encode feedback comments

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** An attacker submits a feedback comment containing HTML or script. In `frontend/src/app/administration/administration.component.ts:91` the comment is marked trusted with `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` and bound to `innerHTML` in the admin table and detail dialog, so the payload runs in the administrator's session and can read the JWT from `localStorage`.

No client-side control exists; server-side feedback sanitization was not inspected.

```typescript
→ 91 │           feedback.comment = this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(feedback.comment)

```

#### F-040 · DOM XSS

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/last-login-ip/last-login-ip.component.ts:39`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Unescaped bypass of the `lastLoginIp` claim is shown, but how an attacker controls that claim is outside the supplied source.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** At `frontend/src/app/last-login-ip/last-login-ip.component.ts:39`, the component decodes the stored JWT, wraps `payload.data.lastLoginIp` in HTML, passes it through the Angular sanitizer bypass and renders it with `innerHTML`. If an attacker can get script markup into the `lastLoginIp` claim, for example through a forwarded client-IP header that the server stores, the script runs in the victim's session and can read the `localStorage` token.

How the server populates the claim was not read.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

```typescript
  37 │       if (payload.data.lastLoginIp) {

  40 │       }
  41 │     }
```

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◑ [M-005](#m-005) — Manual review: verify DOM XSS via trusted last login IP from token · ◕ [M-053](#m-053) — Render last login IP as escaped text

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

<a id="t-022"></a><a id="f-022"></a>
#### F-022 · Lockfiles disabled so every build resolves floating dependencies

**Severity:** 🟠 High  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** `.npmrc:1`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-037](#w-037) - Build pipeline trusts mutable third-party references

**Issue:** The npmrc disables lockfile generation, so CI, release packaging and Docker builds run npm install against floating semver ranges. An attacker who publishes a malicious version of a direct or transitive package, or compromises a maintainer account, gets it installed in release artifacts and in jobs holding secrets, with no diff signal.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

```
→ 1 │ package-lock=false
```

**Fix:** ◕ [M-037](#m-037) — Commit lockfiles and install from them

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-829](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/829.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/)

### 🟡 Medium (52)

<a id="t-088"></a><a id="f-088"></a>
#### F-088 · Hardcoded HMAC key for security answers

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-01](#c-01) - Sequelize application data access  ·  **Location:** `models/securityAnswer.ts:45`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-010](#w-010) - Secrets are committed to source instead of a managed store

**Issue:** Security answers are stored via `security.hmac`, which uses a literal key embedded in source. Anyone with the source and a database copy can precompute HMACs of likely answers and recover them, enabling password reset abuse.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Move the credential out of source control into a secret store and rotate it → ◑ [M-094](#m-094) — Move secrets to a managed secret store (🟡 [F-088](#f-088))

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/)

<a id="t-101"></a><a id="f-101"></a>
#### F-101 · Hardcoded test credentials in client bundle

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/login/login.component.ts:62`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-010](#w-010) - Secrets are committed to source instead of a managed store

**Issue:** The login component ships a test username and password in the client bundle. Any visitor can read them and attempt to authenticate with them.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Move the credential out of source control into a secret store and rotate it → ◑ [M-107](#m-107) — Remove embedded test credentials

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/)

<a id="t-065"></a><a id="f-065"></a>
#### F-065 · Product review author taken from client

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/createProductReviews.ts:26`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** PUT `/rest/products/:id/reviews` has no `isAuthorized` middleware and stores `req.body.author` as-is; the code even detects a mismatch only to flag a challenge. Anyone can post reviews as any user.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-074](#m-074) — Derive review author from verified session

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-290](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/290.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/)

<a id="t-066"></a><a id="f-066"></a>
#### F-066 · Password change accepts any non-empty password

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/changePassword.ts:19`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** A user or an attacker holding a session can set a trivially weak password such as a single character, because the change-password handler only checks that the value is non-empty and matches the repeat value. Weak passwords are then easy to guess.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Enforce a length and complexity policy and reject reused / breached passwords → ◑ [M-075](#m-075) — Enforce a password strength policy server-side

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04)

<a id="t-071"></a><a id="f-071"></a>
#### F-071 · Password reset enforces no password strength policy

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `routes/resetPassword.ts:26`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** The reset handler only checks that the new password is non-empty and matches the repeat. Any weak password is accepted and stored, so accounts are easily brute-forced.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Enforce a length and complexity policy and reject reused / breached passwords → ◑ [M-080](#m-080) — Enforce a server-side password strength policy

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-04](#tc-authn-04)

<a id="t-078"></a><a id="f-078"></a>
#### F-078 · Non-atomic wallet balance check and debit at checkout

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/order.ts:149`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** `placeOrder` reads the wallet balance, compares it with `totalPrice` and then decrements in a separate query. Concurrent checkouts can each pass the check and overdraw the wallet; the model validates only that balance is an integer. Whether UserId is token-bound for this route was not visible.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-084](#m-084) — Debit wallet atomically in a transaction

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-367](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/367.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-079"></a><a id="f-079"></a>
#### F-079 · Fetched profile image stored without type

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/profileImageUrlUpload.ts:30`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-FILE-02](#tc-file-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** The body fetched from the attacker-supplied `imageUrl` is streamed to a public uploads file with no content-type or size limit; an attacker URL ending in .svg stores attacker-controlled SVG served from the app origin, and large bodies fill disk.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Validate uploaded file type, size, and storage path; never execute uploaded content → ◑ [M-085](#m-085) — Validate type and size of fetched profile images

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-434](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/434.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/) · Verification items: [TC-FILE-02](#tc-file-02)

<a id="t-080"></a><a id="f-080"></a>
#### F-080 · CSP header built from user-controlled profile image

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/userProfile.ts:88`

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: `profileImage` is interpolated into the CSP header unescaped, but the write path and validation of `profileImage` are not in the supplied window.

**Issue:** The `Content-Security-Policy` header interpolates `user.profileImage`, which users set via profile image URL upload. A value containing a semicolon and directives such as script-src 'unsafe-inline' weakens the page's own CSP, enabling script execution from a stored username.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** ◑ [M-011](#m-011) — Manual review: verify CSP header built from user-controlled profile image value · ◑ [M-086](#m-086) — Validate profile image before use

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-644](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/644.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-081"></a><a id="f-081"></a>
#### F-081 · Feedback creation trusts UserId from request body

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:402`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** POST `/api/Feedbacks` is open to anonymous callers and passes the body UserId through. An attacker can post feedback attributed to any other user.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-087](#m-087) — Bind feedback UserId to the authenticated caller

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03)

<a id="t-082"></a><a id="f-082"></a>
#### F-082 · Security answer creation accepts owner id from request body

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:501`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-003](#w-003) - Privileged attributes lack an enforced write allowlist

**Issue:** POST `/api/SecurityAnswers` has no authentication and no `appendUserId`, so the finale resource takes UserId from the body. An attacker can create a security answer bound to another user's id.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-088](#m-088) — Bind security answer owner to caller

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-915](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/915.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03)

<a id="t-083"></a><a id="f-083"></a>
#### F-083 · NoSQL operator injection

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/likeProductReviews.ts:25`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-02](#tc-input-02))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: `req.body.id` is used unvalidated as _id filter, but datastore operator handling is not shown.

**Weakness:** [W-006](#w-006) - Document-database queries take their structure from application values

**Issue:** At `routes/likeProductReviews.ts:25`, `req.body.id` is used directly as the _id predicate in `findOne` and update on POST `/rest/products/reviews`. An authenticated user can post an operator object such as `{"$ne":-1}` instead of a string, changing the query semantics so it may match other reviews and modify the like counter of arbitrary reviews.

The datastore's operator handling was not inspected.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** Replace string concatenation in query operators with parameter binding → ◑ [M-012](#m-012) — Manual review: verify NoSQL operator injection via unvalidated review id · ◑ [M-089](#m-089) — Validate review id as a string before querying

**Classification:** Injection · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-02](#tc-input-02)

<a id="t-084"></a><a id="f-084"></a>
#### F-084 · Review update lacks ownership check

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/updateProductReviews.ts:18`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** Any logged-in user sends PATCH `/rest/products/reviews` with an _id operator object. The update uses `multi: true` and never compares the review author to the caller, so the user can overwrite other users' review messages.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Replace string concatenation in query operators with parameter binding → ◑ [M-090](#m-090) — Scope review update to the owning author

**Classification:** Injection · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-085"></a><a id="f-085"></a>
#### F-085 · Stored product reviews fed to model as tool results

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:149`

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Reviews are returned to the model, but attacker write access to reviews is outside the supplied source.

**Weakness:** [W-019](#w-019) - Stored product reviews fed to model as tool results (`chat.ts:149`)

**Issue:** LLM01/ASI01: `getProductReviews` returns stored review text, writable by unauthenticated PUT callers, directly into model context. An attacker plants instructions in a review that later steer another user's chat, including tool calls.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** ◑ [M-013](#m-013) — Manual review: verify Stored product reviews fed to model as tool results · ◑ [M-091](#m-091) — Isolate stored content from model instructions

**Classification:** Injection · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-1427](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1427.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-089"></a><a id="f-089"></a>
#### F-089 · FTP file allow-list bypass

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/fileServer.ts:33`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-FILE-01](#tc-file-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Weakness:** [W-005](#w-005) - Input handling lacks enforced boundary validation

**Issue:** A request to `/ftp/``&lt;name&gt;.bak`%`2500.md` passes the .md/.pdf extension check, then `cutOffPoisonNullByte` strips the suffix and `sendFile` serves a non-allow-listed file from ftp/ such as backups.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Resolve and normalise every constructed path and reject anything that escapes the intended base directory → ◑ [M-095](#m-095) — Validate file name after normalization in FTP server

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-22](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/22.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/) · Verification items: [TC-FILE-01](#tc-file-01)

<a id="t-090"></a><a id="f-090"></a>
#### F-090 · Null byte bypass of public file extension allowlist

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/fileServer.ts:28`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** The `/ftp` file server checks that the name ends in .md or .pdf, then cuts the name at a %00 byte before `sendFile`. An unauthenticated request for a backup file name followed by an encoded null byte and .md passes the check and serves the backup file from ftp/.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-096](#m-096) — Validate file names after null byte handling

**Classification:** Insecure File Handling · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-158](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/158.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/)

<a id="t-091"></a><a id="f-091"></a>
#### F-091 · Captcha answer returned in challenge response

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/captcha.ts:31`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** GET `/rest/captcha` returns the captcha object including its answer, so automated clients solve it trivially and the captcha protects nothing on feedback submission.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Restrict the response to the minimum fields needed and never echo secrets → ◑ [M-097](#m-097) — Omit captcha answer from responses

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/)

<a id="t-092"></a><a id="f-092"></a>
#### F-092 · Current user endpoint exposes fields

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/currentUser.ts:58`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** `/rest/user/whoami` returns any requested `user.data` field including the password hash and supports a JSONP callback. A malicious page loads it as a script from a logged-in victim's browser and reads email and hash cross-origin.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Restrict the response to the minimum fields needed and never echo secrets → ◑ [M-098](#m-098) — Remove JSONP and allowlist returned fields

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/)

<a id="t-093"></a><a id="f-093"></a>
#### F-093 · Unauthenticated memories endpoint returns joined user

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/memory.ts:24`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-028](#w-028) - Unauthenticated memories endpoint returns joined user records (`memory.ts:24`)

**Issue:** GET `/rest/memories` has no authentication and returns every memory with its included UserModel record. A caller can enumerate users who posted memories. Whether sensitive fields such as the password hash are serialized depends on model code not read here.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Restrict the response to the minimum fields needed and never echo secrets → ◑ [M-099](#m-099) — Limit memory response fields and require auth

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/)

<a id="t-094"></a><a id="f-094"></a>
#### F-094 · Security question endpoint reveals

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/securityQuestion.ts:25`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-05](#tc-authn-05))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** An unauthenticated caller queries the security-question endpoint with an email; a known account returns its question while an unknown one returns an empty object, allowing account enumeration.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-100](#m-100) — Return uniform responses for recovery lookups

**Classification:** Error Information Disclosure · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-204](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/204.html) · [OWASP A02:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A02_2025-Security_Misconfiguration/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-05](#tc-authn-05)

<a id="t-095"></a><a id="f-095"></a>
#### F-095 · Unauthenticated access to logs and encryption keys

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:283`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-011](#w-011) - Endpoints are reachable without enforced authentication

**Issue:** The logs directory is listed and its files served with no authentication. The encryptionkeys directory is likewise listed and served unauthenticated. Anyone can download access logs, which may contain tokens in URLs, and key material.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-101](#m-101) — Require authentication on every exposed endpoint

**Classification:** Unauthenticated Management Plane · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-306](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/306.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-096"></a><a id="f-096"></a>
#### F-096 · Order history filtered by lossy masked e-mail

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/orderHistory.ts:17`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** GET `/rest/order-history` lists orders whose email equals the caller's e-mail with vowels masked. Accounts with colliding masked addresses see each other's orders.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-102](#m-102) — Scope order history by user id

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-097"></a><a id="f-097"></a>
#### F-097 · Unauthenticated order lookup by order id

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/trackOrder.ts:18`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** GET `/rest/track-order/:id` has no authentication and no ownership comparison. Anyone who knows or guesses an order id receives that order's stored data.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-103](#m-103) — Require ownership for order tracking

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-098"></a><a id="f-098"></a>
#### F-098 · Order lookup by lossy vowel-masked e-mail in data

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/dataExport.ts:33`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Lossy vowel mask used as order key is observed, but the ability to register a colliding address is not shown.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** At `routes/dataExport.ts:33`, orders are stored and queried under the caller's e-mail with vowels replaced by *. Different addresses that differ only in vowels produce the same key, so a user who registers a colliding variant of a victim's address could receive the victim's orders in order history and data export.

Registration of arbitrary colliding addresses was not shown in the sources read.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** ◑ [M-016](#m-016) — Manual review: verify Order lookup by lossy vowel-masked e-mail in data export · ◑ [M-104](#m-104) — Enforce correct server-side authorization (🟡 [F-098](#f-098))

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-863](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/863.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-099"></a><a id="f-099"></a>
#### F-099 · Passwords sent in GET query string

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/Services/user.service.ts:54`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-033](#w-033) - Passwords sent in GET query string (`user.service.ts:54`)

**Issue:** The change-password call places the current and new passwords in the URL of a GET request. They can be exposed through server and proxy logs, browser history and referrers, and the state change can be triggered cross-site.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-105](#m-105) — Send password change as POST body

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-598](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/598.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/)

<a id="t-100"></a><a id="f-100"></a>
#### F-100 · OAuth access token passed in URL query string

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/Services/user.service.ts:68`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-AUTHN-07](#tc-authn-07))

**Reliability:** High. Probably real: a model review confirmed it, and the survey and verification each found it.

**Weakness:** [W-034](#w-034) - OAuth access token passed in URL query string (frontend/src/app/Services/user.se

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-7](#tb-7) - frontend client application → external · Egress content: The access token leaves the client inside the userinfo request URL.

**Issue:** At `frontend/src/app/Services/user.service.ts:68` the Google access token, obtained through the implicit grant (which returns it in the URL fragment), is appended to the userinfo URL as a query parameter. The token can leak through browser history, logs, proxies, referrers and browser tooling, and anyone who captures it can replay it against Google APIs.

Requests go to `googleapis.com` over HTTPS.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-106](#m-106) — Send tokens in Authorization headers using the code flow

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-598](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/598.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-07](#tc-authn-07)

<a id="t-103"></a><a id="f-103"></a>
#### F-103 · Unbounded wallet address set growth on unauthenticated

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-07](#c-07) - Web3 NFT and Wallet Routes  ·  **Location:** `routes/web3Wallet.ts:16`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-015](#w-015) - Unbounded wallet address set growth on unauthenticated route (`web3Wallet.ts:16`)

**Issue:** An attacker repeatedly POSTs `/rest/web3/walletExploitAddress` with unique arbitrary `walletAddress` values (any type or length). Each value is added to the in-memory `walletsConnected` Set without validation, cap or expiry, so process memory grows until degradation.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Bound the request rate and the per-request resource budget on this endpoint → ◑ [M-109](#m-109) — Validate and bound the wallet address set

**Classification:** Denial of Service · STRIDE: Denial of Service · [CWE-770](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/770.html) · [OWASP A06:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A06_2025-Insecure_Design/)

<a id="t-106"></a><a id="f-106"></a>
#### F-106 · Deluxe role granted without payment

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/deluxe.ts:43`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** A customer POSTs `/rest/deluxe-membership` with a `paymentMode` other than wallet or card. Neither payment branch runs, so the handler still sets role deluxe and issues a deluxe token for free.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Add explicit server-side authorisation checks on every protected route → ◑ [M-110](#m-110) — Enforce server-side authorization

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-285](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/285.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-107"></a><a id="f-107"></a>
#### F-107 · Basket item add checks first BasketId but saves last

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/basketItems.ts:42`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** `addBasketItem` validates `basketIds[0]` against the user`'s basket but builds the item from the last BasketId in the raw body. With a duplicated BasketId key an attacker passes the check with their own id and inserts items into another user'`s basket.

With a duplicated BasketId key an attacker passes the check with their own id and inserts items into another user's basket.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-111](#m-111) — Bind basket id to authenticated user

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-108"></a><a id="f-108"></a>
#### F-108 · Product review author taken from request

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/createProductReviews.ts:26`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01), [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** PUT `/rest/products/:id/reviews` has no `isAuthorized` middleware and stores `req.body.author` as the review author. An attacker posts reviews under any user's e-mail address, even unauthenticated.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-112](#m-112) — Bind review author to authenticated caller

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01), [TC-AUTHZ-03](#tc-authz-03)

<a id="t-109"></a><a id="f-109"></a>
#### F-109 · Basket items readable and deletable by any authenticated user

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:359`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** GET and DELETE `/api/BasketItems/:id` only require a valid JWT, and the generated finale resource looks the item up by id alone. An authenticated user can read or delete other users' basket items by enumerating ids.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-113](#m-113) — Enforce basket item ownership on lookup

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-110"></a><a id="f-110"></a>
#### F-110 · Basket item creation can target another user's basket

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:427`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: `basketItems.ts` check and raw-body duplicate-key parsing are not supplied; the mismatch cannot be confirmed.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An authenticated user posts a JSON body to `/api/BasketItems` with duplicate BasketId keys. The ownership check compares only the first BasketId with the caller's basket, but the item is saved with the last one, so the attacker adds items to another user's basket.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous - Check uses `basketIds[0]` while the saved item uses the last BasketId. Raw-body parser behaviour was not read.

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-017](#m-017) — Manual review: verify Basket item creation can target another user's basket · ◑ [M-114](#m-114) — Bind basket item writes to the session basket

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-111"></a><a id="f-111"></a>
#### F-111 · Privacy request creation not bound to authenticated user

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:444`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** Any authenticated user can POST `/api/PrivacyRequests`. The route is guarded only by `security.isAuthorized()`, which verifies the JWT.

The finale-generated create endpoint therefore takes UserId and `deletionRequested` from the request body, so an attacker can file a data-erasure request on behalf of another user's id.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-115](#m-115) — Bind UserId to the authenticated user with appendUserId

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-112"></a><a id="f-112"></a>
#### F-112 · Deluxe membership granted without verified payment

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/deluxe.ts:43`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** POST `/rest/deluxe-membership` only charges when `paymentMode` is wallet or card. Any other `paymentMode` skips every payment branch and reaches `user.update` that sets the deluxe role, issuing a deluxe token. A wallet-less user also passes the balance check.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-116](#m-116) — Require successful payment before role upgrade

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-841](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/841.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-113"></a><a id="f-113"></a>
#### F-113 · Any logged-in user can delete any feedback

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:361`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** DELETE `/api/Feedbacks/:id` is protected only by `isAuthorized`, which validates the JWT but not the role. Any authenticated customer can delete feedback entries that only an administrator should remove.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-117](#m-117) — Require admin role for feedback deletion

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-862](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/862.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-02](#tc-authz-02)

<a id="t-114"></a><a id="f-114"></a>
#### F-114 · Chatbot coupon limits enforced only by prompt text

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:184`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** LLM06/ASI02: the 10% cap and eligibility rules exist only in the system prompt and a schema description. An attacker prompt-injects the model to call `generateCoupon` with discount 50 or more; the zod schema has no max and execute generates a coupon for any value.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-118](#m-118) — Enforce coupon limits server-side

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-1284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1284.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-115"></a><a id="f-115"></a>
#### F-115 · Chatbot coupon limits enforced only by prompt text

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `routes/chat.ts:184`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AI-01](#tc-ai-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. An automatic check confirmed part of the claim.

**Issue:** An unauthenticated caller posts crafted messages to `/rest/chat` that persuade the model to call `generateCoupon` with an arbitrary discount. The tool schema has no maximum and `execute()` issues a coupon without checking policy, so the 10% cap and eligibility rules are not enforced.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-119](#m-119) — Enforce coupon policy in server-side code

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-1427](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1427.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AI-01](#tc-ai-01)

<a id="t-116"></a><a id="f-116"></a>
#### F-116 · Address update lacks ownership check

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-03](#c-03) - Juice Shop Express API  ·  **Location:** `server.ts:450`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-007](#w-007) - Authorization is implemented route by route

**Issue:** An authenticated user sends PUT `/api/Addresss/``&lt;id&gt;` for another user's address. `appendUserId` only overwrites `body.UserId` with the caller's id, and the generated finale resource updates the row by path id, so the attacker can modify and take over any user's address.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Tie every object lookup to the requesting user's identity and reject cross-tenant references → ◑ [M-120](#m-120) — Bind address updates to the authenticated owner

**Classification:** Broken Access Control · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-639](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/639.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHZ-01](#tc-authz-01)

<a id="t-068"></a><a id="f-068"></a>
#### F-068 · Wide TOTP window and weak second-factor throttling

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `routes/2fa.ts:31`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-012](#w-012) - Wide TOTP window and weak second-factor throttling (`2fa.ts:31`)

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-2](#tb-2) 🌐 External - external → Authentication &amp; Session Surface · Authentication: The finding's evidence line is the interface this boundary cites; CWE-307 breaks its authentication condition.

**Issue:** An attacker with a valid first-factor `tmpToken` guesses TOTP codes against `/rest/2fa/verify`; `epochTolerance` 30 accepts a wide set of codes and the limiter allows 100 attempts per 5 minutes per client, with no per-account lockout.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Apply rate limiting and lock-out thresholds on authentication endpoints → ◑ [M-077](#m-077) — Narrow TOTP window and add account lockout

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-307](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/307.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/)

<a id="t-069"></a><a id="f-069"></a>
#### F-069 · Token verification does not enforce expiry on role checks

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `lib/insecurity.ts:55`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-03](#tc-authn-03))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-004](#w-004) - Authentication relies on hand-rolled token verification

**Issue:** `verify()` uses `jws.verify`, which checks only the signature and not the exp claim. `isAccounting`, `isDeluxe`, `isCustomer` and the 2FA temporary token check go through it. No issuer or audience claim is set or validated either.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-078](#m-078) — Validate exp, iss and aud in token verification

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-345](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/345.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-03](#tc-authn-03)

<a id="t-070"></a><a id="f-070"></a>
#### F-070 · JWT verification does not pin the signing algorithm

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `lib/insecurity.ts:55`

**Found by:** verification ([TC-AUTHN-02](#tc-authn-02))

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-004](#w-004) - Authentication relies on hand-rolled token verification

**Issue:** `verify()` calls `jws.verify` with only the token and public key. The server never fixes the accepted algorithm, so the token header influences how the signature is checked. A crafted token could exploit an algorithm mismatch if the library accepts it.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Pin the signature algorithm explicitly and reject `alg:none` and unknown algorithms → ◑ [M-079](#m-079) — Pin RS256 when verifying tokens

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-AUTHN-02](#tc-authn-02)

<a id="t-102"></a><a id="f-102"></a>
#### F-102 · Password hash embedded in signed JWT payload

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-08](#c-08) - Authentication & Session Surface  ·  **Location:** `routes/login.ts:24`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** The full user record including the password hash and TOTP secret fields is signed into the JWT; any token holder or XSS/log exposure reveals the hash for offline cracking, as JWT payloads are only base64-encoded.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-108](#m-108) — Limit JWT claims to minimal identifiers

**Classification:** Cryptographic Failures · STRIDE: Information Disclosure · [CWE-201](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/201.html) · [OWASP A04:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A04_2025-Cryptographic_Failures/)

<a id="t-067"></a><a id="f-067"></a>
#### F-067 · OAuth implicit flow without state or nonce

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/login/login.component.ts:148`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-024](#w-024) - OAuth sign-in requests are not bound to the user's browser session

**Issue:** The Google login redirects with `response_type=token` and no state or nonce parameter. An attacker can induce a victim's browser to complete a login with an attacker-supplied token, and the access token is exposed in the URL fragment.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Enforce a same-origin or signed CSRF token on every state-changing endpoint → ◑ [M-076](#m-076) — Add anti-CSRF protection to state-changing requests

**Classification:** Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF) · STRIDE: Spoofing · [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html) · [OWASP A01:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A01_2025-Broken_Access_Control/)

<a id="t-072"></a><a id="f-072"></a>
#### F-072 · Product description stored without sanitization

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-01](#c-01) - Sequelize application data access  ·  **Location:** `models/product.ts:45`

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Setter skips sanitization when the challenge is enabled (config-dependent), but the write route and rendering sink were not shown, so exploitability is unproven.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** When the restful XSS challenge is enabled, the description setter skips sanitization and stores raw input. An attacker able to write products can persist script markup that executes in viewers' browsers. Rendering sink was not read.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◑ [M-010](#m-010) — Manual review: verify Product description stored without sanitization · ◑ [M-081](#m-081) — Always sanitize product description input

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/)

<a id="t-086"></a><a id="f-086"></a>
#### F-086 · Stored XSS

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/search-result/search-result.component.ts:110`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: Trust bypass on product descriptions is shown, but the rendering sink and ability to store markup are outside the supplied window.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** At `frontend/src/app/search-result/search-result.component.ts:110`, product descriptions from the API are marked as trusted HTML and shown with `innerHTML`. If an attacker can store markup in a description, the script executes in every visitor's browser.

The rendering sink lies in the product component, which was not read, and server-side write protections were not inspected.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◑ [M-014](#m-014) — Manual review: verify Stored XSS via product descriptions trusted as HTML · ◑ [M-092](#m-092) — Stop trusting API product description HTML

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

<a id="t-087"></a><a id="f-087"></a>
#### F-087 · XSS

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-05](#c-05) - frontend client application  ·  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/track-result/track-result.component.ts:48`

**Found by:** survey and verification ([TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04))

**Reliability:** Low. Not confirmed, this may be a false positive: `orderId` is wrapped and trusted as HTML, but the template binding and whether the server echoes the query id unchanged are not shown.

**Weakness:** [W-008](#w-008) - Frontend rendering lacks enforced output encoding

**Issue:** At `frontend/src/app/track-result/track-result.component.ts:48`, the tracking result wraps the server-returned `orderId` in markup, marks it trusted and binds it with `innerHtml`. If the server echoes attacker-supplied input from the id query parameter, an attacker can send a link with a script id and run it in the victim's browser.

Whether the server echoes the id unchanged was not verified.

**Evidence:** ◌ ambiguous

**Fix:** Output-encode untrusted strings at every sink and remove all `bypassSecurityTrustHtml` calls → ◑ [M-015](#m-015) — Manual review: verify XSS via order id trusted as HTML in order tracking · ◑ [M-093](#m-093) — Render order number as escaped text

**Classification:** Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html) · [OWASP A05:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A05_2025-Injection/) · Verification items: [TC-INPUT-04](#tc-input-04)

<a id="t-073"></a><a id="f-073"></a>
#### F-073 · Missing Container Image Signing

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml:1`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-004](#w-004) - Authentication relies on hand-rolled token verification

**Issue:** Missing Container Image Signing: Unsigned container images cannot be verified for provenance - any registry intermediary could substitute them.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Pin the signature algorithm explicitly and reject `alg:none` and unknown algorithms → ◑ [M-018](#m-018) — Sign and verify release artifacts

**Classification:** Broken Authentication · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html) · [OWASP A07:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A07_2025-Authentication_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-SUPPLY-04](#tc-supply-04) · Rules: IAC-040

<a id="t-074"></a><a id="f-074"></a>
#### F-074 · Heroku CLI installed by piping remote script to shell

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml:358`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** The deploy job downloads an install script with curl and executes it directly in the same job that later uses the Heroku API key. Whoever controls that endpoint or tampers with the transfer can run code in the deploy job and reach the production deployment credential.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-082](#m-082) — Verify the Heroku CLI download before execution

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-494](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/494.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/)

<a id="t-075"></a><a id="f-075"></a>
#### F-075 · Untrusted npm Install/Postinstall Scripts Enabled

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (2)

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-027](#w-027) - Untrusted npm Install/Postinstall Scripts Enabled

**Instances (2):** `Dockerfile`: 4; `package.json`: 56

**Issue:** Dependency Install Scripts Run at Build Time: Without `--ignore-scripts`, any compromised dependency publisher can execute postinstall code at build time.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-019](#m-019) — Disable untrusted package install scripts

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-506](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/506.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-SUPPLY-03](#tc-supply-03) · Rules: IAC-005

<a id="t-076"></a><a id="f-076"></a>
#### F-076 · GitHub Actions referenced by mutable tag or branch

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium - elevated to High because verified finding evidence ties the weakness to confirmed internet ingress tb-3  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (3)

**Found by:** survey and scanners

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-037](#w-037) - Build pipeline trusts mutable third-party references

**Trust boundary gap:** [tb-3](#tb-3) 🌐 External - external → CI/CD Pipeline · Validation: The finding's evidence line is the interface this boundary cites; CWE-829 breaks its validation condition.<br>_⬆ Confirmed internet ingress at tb-3 raised this finding: Medium → High._

**Instances (3):** `.github/workflows/image_actions.yml`: 33; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: 188; `.github/workflows/codeql-analysis.yml`: 23

**Issue:** Workflows reference third-party actions by mutable refs. `.github/workflows/image_actions.yml:33` runs calibreapp/image-actions from the main branch and passes it the `GITHUB_TOKEN`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml:188` uses coverallsapp/github-action by tag v2 with the token, and CodeQL actions by tag v3; `.github/workflows/codeql-analysis.yml:23` was also flagged for tag-based references.

The finding holds three places in total.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◕ [M-083](#m-083) — Pin third-party dependencies to immutable versions

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-829](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/829.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-SUPPLY-01](#tc-supply-01) · Rules: IAC-011

<a id="t-077"></a><a id="f-077"></a>
#### F-077 · Container base images referenced by tag instead of digest

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (3)

**Found by:** survey and scanners

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-026](#w-026) - Container base images referenced by tag instead of digest (`Dockerfile:22`, Docker

**Instances (3):** `Dockerfile`: 22, 1; `test/smoke/Dockerfile`: 1

**Issue:** `Dockerfile:1` builds from the tag-only image node:24, and `Dockerfile:22` uses a distroless tag; the smoke test `Dockerfile` uses unpinned alpine. A replaced or compromised upstream image would flow into the published bkimminich/juice-shop image pushed by CI.

The runtime image is distroless and runs as non-root user 65532.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** Replace the unmaintained dependency with a maintained equivalent or fork it under ownership → ◑ [M-020](#m-020) — Pin the container base image to an immutable digest

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Tampering · [CWE-1104](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1104.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-SUPPLY-01](#tc-supply-01) · Rules: IAC-001

<a id="t-104"></a><a id="f-104"></a>
#### F-104 · Container Runs as Root

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** `test/smoke/Dockerfile:1`

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Issue:** Container Runs as Root: Containers running as root expand container-escape blast radius.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-021](#m-021) — Drop unnecessary privileges in build and runtime

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-250](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/250.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-INFRA-01](#tc-infra-01) · Rules: IAC-002

<a id="t-105"></a><a id="f-105"></a>
#### F-105 · Missing Workflow Permissions Block

**Severity:** 🟡 Medium  ·  **Component:** [C-02](#c-02) - CI/CD Pipeline  ·  **Location:** Multiple locations (13)

**Reliability:** Medium. A model review confirmed it, but the survey and verification did not both find it.

**Weakness:** [W-023](#w-023) - Missing Workflow Permissions Block

**Instances (13):** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: 25; `.github/workflows/frontend-bundle-analysis.yml`: 13; `.github/workflows/image_actions.yml`: 21; `.github/workflows/lint-fixer.yml`: 6; `.github/workflows/rebase.yml`: 8; `.github/workflows/release.yml`: 10; `.github/workflows/stale.yml`: 7; `.github/workflows/update-challenges-ebook.yml`: 11 … (+5 more)

**Issue:** Missing Workflow Permissions Block: Without an explicit permissions block at workflow or job level, the job inherits the repository default (commonly write-all) for `GITHUB_TOKEN` - any compromised step can push code, create releases, or approve PRs.

**Evidence:** ✓ verified

**Fix:** ◑ [M-022](#m-022) — Apply least-privilege permissions

**Classification:** Supply-Chain Integrity · STRIDE: Elevation of Privilege · [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html) · [OWASP A03:2025](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A03_2025-Software_Supply_Chain_Failures/) · Verification items: [TC-CI-01](#tc-ci-01) · Rules: IAC-010

_**Reliability** says how sure this report is that a finding is real, not how severe it is. It is a judgement, not a probability._

Step 2 inconclusive<br/><details><summary>Reasons</summary>Step 2: Roles are read from the decoded token (`data.role`) with no database re-fetch, as in isCustomer.
